{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"shaves","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"shaving","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"shaved","tags":["past"]},{"form":"shove","tags":["obsolete","past"]},{"form":"shaved","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"shove","tags":["obsolete","participle","past"]},{"form":"shaven","tags":["participle","past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To make (the head, skin etc.) bald or (the hair) shorter by using a tool such as a razor or electric clippers to cut the hair close to the skin."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To cut anything in this fashion."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I had little time to shave this morning.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To remove hair from one's face by this means."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To cut finely, for example slices of meat."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To skim along or near the surface of; to pass close to, or touch lightly, in passing."]},{"glosses":["To reduce in size, weight, time taken etc., usually by a small amount."]},{"glosses":["To be hard and severe in a bargain with; to practice extortion on; to cheat."],"tags":["archaic","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To buy (a note) at a discount greater than the legal rate of interest, or to deduct in discounting it more than the legal rate allows."],"tags":["US","dated","slang","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To injure by employing a knife."],"synonyms":[{"word":"stab"},{"word":"chef"},{"word":"ching"},{"word":"juice"},{"word":"shiv"},{"word":"jook"},{"word":"shank"}],"tags":["Multicultural-London-English","slang","transitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈʃeɪ̯v/"},{"ipa":"[ˈʃeɪ̯v]"}],"upstream_line":251439,"upstream_offset":1247544942,"upstream_record_sha256":"258b6aeb1be912af9805d36dbc3f87acb2a2736a48a6a191a7973e785e01c8e7","word":"shave"}
